# Agents · D · part 9

953 entries, developer-child to devops-engineer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **developer-child**: Implement a scoped task delegated by a stack developer skill. Do not edit PLAN or session state. ([tibursocampos/agent-dev-toolkit](https://github.com/tibursocampos/agent-dev-toolkit/blob/HEAD/core/agents/developer-child.md) · ★6)
- **developer-codex**: DEFAULT developer. Delegates to Codex (frontier GPT, reasoning xhigh; model from ~/.codex/config.toml) via MCP. Saves Claude tokens — use aggressively given corporate Codex subscription. GPT xhigh reasoning is top-tier; quality comparable to Senior for well-specified tasks. Main constraint: receives context via prompt rather than live filesystem access, so spec must have explicit file paths and clear requirements. Prefer this over Claude developers unless task requires broad codebase exploration or has genuinely ambiguous scope that the feature skill couldn't fully specify. ([roman-karpovich/ai-dev-team](https://github.com/roman-karpovich/ai-dev-team/blob/HEAD/agents/developer-codex.md) · ★1)
- **developer-community-manager**: Use to diagnose and grow a developer community — measure health by answered-question rate and returning contributors (not follower count), design forum/Discord ops, and build contributor/ambassador programs. NOT for demand-gen social (marketing-operations) or reference docs (technical-writing-docs). ([mcorbett51090/RavenClaude](https://github.com/mcorbett51090/RavenClaude/blob/HEAD/plugins/developer-relations/agents/developer-community-manager.md) · ★7)
- **developer-csharp**: Use this agent when writing, reviewing, or refactoring C# / .NET code. Produces production-ready C# for ASP.NET Core 8+ Web APIs, minimal APIs, and worker services. Tooling: `dotnet format`, `dotnet test` (xUnit), Roslyn analyzers, EditorConfig. Typical… ([luketherose/claude-registry](https://github.com/luketherose/claude-registry/blob/HEAD/gemini/extensions/dev-standards/agents/developer-csharp.md))
